SELECT `i`.`insurance_provider_id` AS `provider_id`, `i`.`name` AS `provider_name`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL THEN 1
END) AS `total_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN 1
END) AS `approved_claims`, COUNT(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN 1
END) AS `denied_claims`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'approved') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `approved_amount`, COALESCE(SUM(CASE
    WHEN `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NOT NULL AND (`i0`.`status` = 'denied') THEN `i0`.`claim_amount_submitted`
END), 0.0) AS `denied_amount`
FROM `insurance_provider` AS `i`
LEFT JOIN `insurance_claims` AS `i0` ON `i`.`insurance_provider_id` = `i0`.`insurance_provider_id`
WHERE `i0`.`insurance_claims_id` IS NULL OR ((`i0`.`submmited_date` >= @__start_0) AND (`i0`.`submmited_date` <= @__end_1))
GROUP BY `i`.`insurance_provider_id`, `i`.`name`
